# WF-001 installed released surface census

Companion to the [research decision](wf-001-released-package-contract-audit.md). This is a static research index, not runtime qualification, a support matrix, or a certification gate.

## Reading the census

Each named declaration has exactly one primary disposition and capability ID from the research note. HTTP means a candidate use-case boundary for WF-008, not an approved route. Constructors, values, Views, exceptions and helper methods do not independently become endpoints. Worker-message payloads and schema presence grant no public exposure.

The member column lists directly declared public methods, properties (including promoted properties), constants and enum cases. Inherited members are owned by their declaring base/trait and are not duplicated. Enum engine-provided methods are not source declarations. Exact signatures, imports, inheritance, protected hydration and method-level internal annotations remain at the linked immutable source; this index does not override them.

Public visibility is not a stability promise. X01 marks type-level `@internal`; other classes can also have private/internal members. Deprecated flags reflect declaration PHPDoc, not every deprecation mentioned by documentation. X02 means not selected for this Yii composition, not that the package cannot support another framework.

## Counts

| Artifact scope | PHP files | Named declarations | Direct public members |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| fight-common | 670 | 689 | 3072 |
| fight-access-control | 436 | 435 | 1745 |

Common includes 656 production `src/` files plus 14 Composer-exported `tests/TestCase/` support files (21 declarations). AccessControl includes 428 production files and eight opt-in `openapi/` files (seven anchors plus the declaration-free bootstrap). All 1,106 files parse; all 1,124 named declarations and 4,817 direct public members are accounted for below. The 13 Common functions are separately indexed from their shared file. No top-level `const` declarations were found.
